Departing from 3D morphable models (3DMMs), VDAs use a learned expression space that lifts the expressiveness ceiling of 3DMMs, e.g., for the tongue and mouth interior. However, learned expression codes exhibit heavy viewpoint bias, as our DISTRACT benchmark reveals. We show that this bias can be removed through disentanglement, enabling their use as driving signals for multi-view consistent avatars. Camera and upper-body pose are controlled jointly via proxy-mesh renderings, giving independent control over expression, pose, and viewpoint. A camera-triplet training strategy makes the approach efficient: our models train for 34 hours on a single A100 using only public multi-view data. VDAs outperform state-of-the-art single-image 3D avatars on novel-view reenactment, reducing face LPIPS by 29% and improving perceptual quality (JOD) by 66% on BecomingLit. A static 3DGS reconstruction probe further confirms that our generations are the most multi-view consistent. Built on a video prior, VDAs produce effects beyond the reach of explicit renderers, including physically plausible hair dynamics, shadows, reflections, and refractions.

</details>


### [102] [Revealing the Impact of Visual Text Style on Attribute-based Descriptions Produced by Large Visual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27553)
*Xiaomeng Wang,Martha Larson,Zhengyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉文本风格（功能性可读风格 vs. 装饰性展示风格）是否会影响大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）对文本所指概念在属性层面的描述，发现即使模型正确识别了概念，文本风格仍会"泄漏"进语义推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阅读文字时其含义本应与书写/渲染风格无关，但LVLM在处理图像中的文字时是否会受视觉风格干扰尚不清楚，这对基于LVLM的多媒体系统的评估与改进有重要意义。

Method: 将文本以图像形式呈现（视觉文本），对比功能性风格（如黑色无衬线、以可读性为导向）与装饰性风格（如彩色手写体、以展示为导向），在LVLM能够正确识别文本所指概念的前提下，考察其对该概念属性描述的变化。

Result: 实验结果表明显著存在风格影响：即便概念被正确识别，文本风格仍会系统性地改变模型给出的属性描述，即出现非平凡的风格向语义推断的"泄漏"。

Conclusion: 文本视觉风格会非平凡地泄漏到LVLM的语义推断中，因此面向LVLM的多媒体系统需要开展风格感知的评估与缓解措施。

Abstract: When the visual style of text is considered, a wide variety can be observed in font, color, and size. However, when a word is read, its meaning is independent of the style in which it has been written or rendered. In this paper, we investigate whether, and how, the style in which a word is visualized in an image impacts the description that a Large Visual Language Model (LVLM) provides for the concept to which that word refers. Specifically, we investigate how functional text styles (readability-oriented, e.g., black sans-serif) versus decorative styles (display-oriented, e.g., colored cursive/script) affect LVLMs' descriptions of a concept in terms of the attributes of that concept. Our experiments study the situation in which the LVLM is able to correctly identify the concept referred to by a visual text, i.e., by a word or words rendered as an image, and in which the visual text style should not influence the attribute-based description that the LVLM produces. Our experimental results reveal that even when the concept is correctly identified, text style influences the model's attribute-based descriptions of the concept. Our findings demonstrate non-trivial style leakage from text style into semantic inference and motivate style-aware evaluation and mitigation for LVLM-based multimedia systems.

</details>


### [103] [The Collection of a Real-World, Large-Scale Dataset for Automated Skill Evaluation in Central Line Dressing Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23789)
*Ummey Tanin,Shannan K. Hamlin,Hsin-Mei Chen,Peizhu,Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于中心静脉导管敷料更换（CLDC）技能评估的大规模视频数据集，包含365段注册护士考核录像、17个子任务的时间标注和临床评分，并通过时空深度学习基准实验验证了视频技能评估的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心静脉导管相关血流感染（CLABSI）是可预防的致病与致死来源，规范的CLDC操作对感染防控至关重要；但当前能力评估主要依赖临床教育者人工观察，耗时、难以规模化，且一致性和细粒度不足。AI辅助评估有望更客观、可复现、可扩展，然而进展受限于缺乏大规模、具临床基础的数据集。

Method: 在休斯顿卫理公会医院2026年度能力考核期间采集365段注册护士CLDC操作视频，配套17个子任务级别的时间标注与临床绩效评分；详细描述数据采集与整理流程，包括录制配置试点测试、标注协议设计及采集后处理；并以时空深度学习模型开展基准实验。

Result: 构建了包含365段视频、覆盖17个子任务时间标注与临床绩效评估的数据集；基准实验初步证明基于视频的技能评估在技术上可行。

Conclusion: 该数据集为自动化临床技能评估、反馈生成以及护理教育中的AI辅助培训研究提供了基础，有望推动更客观、可复现、可扩展的临床能力评价。

Abstract: Central line-associated bloodstream infections (CLABSIs) remain a major source of preventable morbidity and mortality in healthcare settings. Proper execution of the central line dressing change (CLDC) procedure is essential for infection prevention, yet current approaches to competency assessment rely primarily on manual ob- servation by clinical educators, making evaluation time-intensive, difficult to scale, and limited in consistency and granularity. These challenges highlight the need for AI-assisted skill evaluation methods that can support more objective, reproducible, and scalable assessment of clinical performance. However, progress in this area has been constrained by the lack of broad-scale, clinically grounded datasets for developing and evaluating such methods. In this work, we present the collection process of a large-scale video dataset for CLDC skill evaluation, comprising 365 recordings of registered nurses collected during the 2026 annual competency exam at Houston Methodist Hospital. Each video is paired with subtask-level temporal annotations and clinical performance assessments spanning 17 subtasks of the procedure. We describe the data collection and curation pipeline, including pilot testing of record- ing configurations, annotation protocol design, and post-collection processing. We further establish the feasibility of video-based skill assessment through benchmark experiments with spatiotemporal deep learning models. This dataset provides a foundation for future research on automated clinical skill assessment, feedback generation, and AI-assisted training in nursing education.

</details>


### [104] [Persistent Planar Memory for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23795)
*Yuze He,Bin Tan,Zelin Gao,Yujun Shen,Yong-Jin Liu,Nan Xue,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PPMem 是一种以"平面基元"作为持久 3D 记忆的视频世界模型，通过联合生成 RGB 视频与平面几何并融合为紧凑的场景平面记忆，实现对数百帧、大视角变化下的一致性生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模生成与探索环境的视频世界模型需要持久的三维记忆来保持一致性。逐像素点云等现有表示过于庞大，而平面基元能以极少参数（每基元7个）紧凑刻画室内外场景的主要结构元素（墙、地板、立面），因此是理想的记忆载体。

Method: 在视频扩散 Transformer 上增加平面预测头与 Depth Token Fusion (DTF) 模块；每个自回归片段同时生成 RGB 视频与平面几何，并将几何合并为持久的平面记忆，再通过渲染深度条件化后续片段；记忆在生成过程中由合并过程不断精化。

Result: 在 DL3DV 与 RealEstate10K 上，视频质量优于先前的 3D-grounded 世界模型，同时输出度量尺度深度、表面法线与可导出的平面几何；在数百帧、大视角变化下保持连贯。记忆相比逐像素点云紧凑约三个数量级（约 10 万个基元、整场景约 5MB）。

Conclusion: 平面基元作为持久 3D 记忆是一种紧凑、几何显式、跨视角一致且可自适应的有效表示，为长时程视频世界模型提供了可行的场景记忆方案。

Abstract: Video world models that generate and explore environments at scale need a persistent 3D memory of what they have produced. Plane primitives offer a natural candidate: a compact set of oriented planes captures the dominant structural elements (e.g., walls, floors, facades) of indoor and outdoor scenes, while requiring only 7 parameters per primitive. We present PPMem, a video world model that adopts plane primitives as its persistent 3D memory. PPMem augments a video diffusion transformer with a planar prediction head and a Depth Token Fusion (DTF) module: each autoregressive chunk jointly produces RGB video and planar geometry, which merges into a persistent plane memory that conditions subsequent chunks via rendered depth. The resulting memory is three orders of magnitude more compact than per-pixel point clouds (~100K primitives, 5MB for a full scene), geometrically explicit (metric depth, normals, exportable surfaces), cross-view consistent (jointly predicted by the shared backbone), and adaptive (refined by the merging procedure as generation proceeds). On DL3DV and RealEstate10K, PPMem outperforms prior 3D-grounded world models in video quality while jointly producing metric-scale depth, surface normals, and exportable planar geometry, sustaining coherence across hundreds of frames under large viewpoint changes. Project page: https://hyzcluster.github.io/ppmem.

</details>


### [105] [StopWatch: Goal Grounding for Object-goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23841)
*Xinhao Liu,Yunsheng Tian,Yuheng Qiu,Pieter Abbeel,Xi Chen,Guanya Shi,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文诊断出物体目标导航中预训练VLM微调策略的主要失败原因是"目标 grounding"（目标定位/距离估计），并显式加入目标 grounding，在三个场景集合的六个基准上达到SOTA，同时修正了基准中的标注缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将VLM微调为导航策略已成为常见做法，尽管有丰富的世界知识和语义先验，成功率仍然偏低，且不清楚这些微调策略究竟缺少哪种能力。作者希望找出主导失败因素。

Method: 通过失败分析诊断出目标 grounding 是主导失败模式（策略探索能力尚可，但常常没注意到目标已在视野中，或无法估计目标距离）。据此在策略中显式地对目标进行 grounding。此外还修正了所用基准中的标注缺陷，以便更忠实地评估后续方法。

Result: 显式 grounding 显著提升成功率，在三个场景集合的六个基准上取得SOTA。在修正后的干净基准上分析表明：给定真值 grounding 时，该策略几乎与生成其演示数据的专家持平，说明剩余差距位于感知而非策略学习。

Conclusion: 目标 grounding 是物体目标导航中被忽视的关键缺失环节；显式 grounding 提供了一个简单而有效的解决方案，并指出未来提升空间在于感知能力。

Abstract: Pretrained VLMs bring rich world knowledge and semantic priors to object-goal navigation, and fine-tuning a VLM into a navigation policy has become a common recipe. Despite this progress, success rates remain low, and it is unclear which capability these fine-tuned policies lack. Our diagnosis identifies goal grounding as the dominant failure. These policies explore well, yet often fail to notice that the goal is already in view or to estimate how far away it is. To address this failure, we ground the goal explicitly. Explicit grounding substantially improves success and reaches state-of-the-art results on six benchmarks across three scene collections. We also correct annotation defects in these benchmarks, so that future methods are evaluated more faithfully. Our analysis on the clean benchmarks shows that, with ground-truth grounding, our policy nearly matches the expert that generated its demonstrations, leaving the headroom in perception rather than policy learning. Our work points to goal grounding as a missing piece of object-goal navigation and offers a simple and effective solution to the task.

</details>


### [106] [Rethinking the design space of Vanilla Pixel Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23854)
*Soumik Mukhopadhyay,Long Mai,Abhinav Shrivastava,Aniruddha Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MFlow，一种在数据主方向上自适应插值 x0-预测与 u-预测的参数化方法，并发现模型容量越大越偏好速度预测；同时提出后置训练轻量卷积模块ConvRefiner修复像素扩散模型的高频缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散模型可避免潜空间瓶颈与独立自编码器的有损压缩，JiT表明朴素扩散Transformer用 x0-预测即可在像素空间生成。但 x0-与 u-预测的取舍此前是固定选择，缺乏对其随数据表示与模型容量变化的系统理解；此外像素扩散生成图像仍存在高频细节不足的问题。

Method: 1) MFlow：学习在数据主方向上于 x0-预测和 u-预测之间自适应插值的参数化，与扩散训练端到端联合学习；2) 分析参数化偏好随数据表示与模型容量的变化规律；3) ConvRefiner：在冻结扩散骨干的前提下，以对抗损失后置训练一个轻量卷积模块，用于恢复高频统计特性。

Result: 在ImageNet、UCF-101视频以及大规模文生图任务上，MFlow取得有竞争力或更优的生成性能；偏好分析显示增大模型容量会使偏好向速度预测偏移。朴素像素扩散Transformer（无论何种参数化）相对真实图像存在持续的高频缺失，ConvRefiner仅需少量额外训练即可恢复高频统计并保持FID。

Conclusion: 参数化选择应随数据表示与模型容量自适应，而非固定；像素扩散的高频缺陷可在不重训骨干的情况下由轻量对抗后置模块有效弥补。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion models have recently re-emerged as a compelling alternative to latent diffusion, avoiding the lossy latent bottleneck and separately trained autoencoder. JiT shows that a vanilla diffusion transformer can generate directly in pixel space by replacing the commonly used velocity prediction ($\boldsymbol{u}$-prediction) with clean image prediction ($\boldsymbol{x_0}$-prediction). In this work, we revisit this choice and introduce MFlow, an adaptive parameterization that learns to interpolate between $\boldsymbol{x_0}$- and $\boldsymbol{u}$-prediction across principal directions of the data, learned end-to-end with diffusion training. We find that the preferred parameterization varies systematically with the data representation and model capacity -- notably, increasing model capacity shifts preference toward velocity prediction. We evaluate MFlow across ImageNet, UCF-101 video, and large-scale text-to-image generation, demonstrating competitive or improved generative performance across diverse settings. In addition, we find that vanilla pixel diffusion transformers, irrespective of parameterization, retain a persistent high-frequency deficit relative to real images. To address this, we introduce ConvRefiner, a lightweight convolutional module trained post-hoc with an adversarial loss while keeping the diffusion backbone frozen. With only a small amount of additional training, ConvRefiner restores the high-frequency statistics of generated images while preserving FID. Project webpage: https://soumik-kanad.github.io/mflow-diffusion/.

</details>


### [107] [Learning to Correct Geometry in Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23856)
*Weijie Lyu,Tiancheng Shen,Jason Li,Yujing Wang,Xiaoyuan Wang,Haobo Yuan,Yizhou Zhao,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视频生成在相机运动下常丢失几何一致性，作者认为根源在于训练数据缺乏视角变化监督，据此提出基于合成配对数据的后处理视频到视频矫正模型VGC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络视频中大部分可见运动来自静态/弱运动相机拍摄的动态物体，针对视角变化引起的外观变化监督信号稀疏，导致生成视频在相机运动下结构扭曲、视差不一致、平滑运镜退化为镜头切换。作者将这一失败归因于数据问题。

Method: 从具有多样相机运动和位姿标注的真实视频出发，构建VG-Pairs合成配对监督生成流程：每个视频片段作为几何一致的目标，用预训练图像到视频修复模型结合轨迹自适应关键帧合成失真版本。VGC学习将失真输入映射到几何一致目标，不修改源生成器，并以光流作为辅助线索解耦相机运动与物体运动。

Result: 在位姿条件与提示驱动两类基准上，VGC在多个源生成器上均提升了几何一致性；扩展到更大骨干网络后，框架可进一步面向更通用的视频精修任务。

Conclusion: 几何一致性问题在很大程度上是数据监督不足所致，通过合成配对监督训练轻量后处理模型即可在不动源生成器的前提下有效改善相机运动下的几何一致性，且具备随骨干规模扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Generated videos often lose geometric consistency under camera motion: structures warp under viewpoint change, parallax becomes inconsistent, and smooth camera movement may collapse into shot-like transitions. We argue that this failure is largely a data problem. In web-scale video corpora, most visible motion comes from dynamic objects captured by static or weakly moving cameras, leaving only sparse supervision for viewpoint-induced appearance changes. To address this, we propose Video Geometry Corrector (VGC), a post-hoc video-to-video model trained on synthetic paired supervision. Starting from real videos with diverse camera motion and pose annotations, we construct VG-Pairs with a synthetic paired-supervision data generation pipeline. Each video clip is treated as a geometry-consistent target and paired with a distorted counterpart synthesized by a pretrained image-to-video inpainting model using trajectory-adaptive keyframes. VGC learns to map the distorted input to the geometry-consistent target without modifying the source generator, and uses optical flow as an auxiliary cue to help disentangle camera motion from object motion. Experiments on pose-conditioned and prompt-driven benchmarks show that VGC improves geometric consistency across multiple source generators, and scaling to a larger backbone further extends the framework toward more general video refinement.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Embeddings Are Overpriced? A Frozen 16 MB Vocabulary for Small and Ternary Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22428)
*Sai Kiran Bathula*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文用一张由预训练嵌入经主成分投影与符号二值化得到的、无训练参数的512比特冻结码表（16 MB）替代小型语言模型中的大型嵌入表，并在相同数据与算力下从头训练同构Gemma模型进行对照。结果显示学习型嵌入表仅换来0.20 nats的验证损失优势，但代价是2.6倍可训练参数；在下游任务上冻结表模型持平或更好，且在稀有词、新词扩展和端侧推理速度/内存上显著占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小型语言模型（如Gemma 3 270M）中，词表嵌入是参数量最大的单一组件（168M/268M），且量化方法通常将嵌入保留为16位，导致其在压缩后仍占据文件主要体积。作者因此质疑：这张昂贵的嵌入表究竟带来了多少实际收益？

Method: 将可训练嵌入表替换为一张512比特码的冻结表（16 MB、零可训练参数），该表由预训练嵌入经主成分投影和符号二值化一次性导出。使用相同Gemma结构的主体、相同的2.05B token数据，分别在有无该冻结表的情况下从头训练并对比；进一步将主体换成三元BitNet b1.58，考察整模型压缩与端侧CPU推理表现。

Result: 学习型嵌入表以2.6倍可训练参数换取0.20 nats的验证损失；但在LAMBADA、HellaSwag、PIQA上冻结表模型更好或持平，仅ARC-Easy偏好学习表。配合三元BitNet主体，整模型为46 MB文件，在LAMBADA与PIQA上与浮点模型持平；笔记本CPU上以62 MB内存实现615–740 tokens/s，速度是llama.cpp中8位GGUF浮点模型的5–6倍，内存仅为其1/7。冻结表在稀有词上领先1.7–2.3 nats，训练后可低成本接纳10,000个新词（代价3%），而学习表则崩溃达43%。全部权重、数据与评测答案均已开源。

Conclusion: 小型模型的嵌入表参数开销远大于其带来的性能收益；基于主成分投影加符号二值化的冻结码表在保持下游性能的同时，大幅降低参数量、文件体积与内存占用，并提升推理速度与词表可扩展性，为端侧小模型提供了一种更经济的词表表示方案。

Abstract: In a small language model the vocabulary is the single largest part. In Gemma 3 270M the embedding table holds 168M of 268M parameters, and because quantisation methods leave the embedding in 16-bit, it is also most of the file once the rest of the model is compressed. We ask what that table actually buys. We replace it with one frozen table of 512-bit codes, 16 MB, with no trainable parameters, derived once from a pretrained embedding by principal-component projection and sign binarisation, and we train the same Gemma-shaped body from scratch on the same 2.05B tokens with and without it. The learned table buys 0.20 nats of validation loss for 2.6 times the trainable parameters, but on LAMBADA, HellaSwag and PIQA the frozen-table model is better or tied; only ARC-Easy favours the learned table. With a ternary BitNet b1.58 body the whole model is a 46 MB file, still ties the float model on LAMBADA and PIQA, and on a laptop CPU generates 615 to 740 tokens per second in 62 MB of memory, five to six times the speed of the float model's 8-bit GGUF in llama.cpp at a seventh of the memory. The frozen table wins by 1.7 to 2.3 nats on rare words, accepts 10,000 new words after training with a 3% cost where the learned table collapses by 43%, and all of it is released with the weights, the data and every evaluation answer.

</details>


### [109] [Where Prefill Expert Reduction Hurts MoE Models: A Few Prompt Tokens Carry Most of the Short-Answer F1 Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22454)
*Zhiyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在预填充阶段让提示词token只经过更少的专家路由，虽能降低MoE模型的服务成本，但会损害答案形式而非答案内容：损伤集中在提示词末端的chat-template标记（Qwen3）和起始处的指令token（DeepSeek-V2-Lite），保持这些敏感位置使用全部专家即可将F1损失控制在1分以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少预填充阶段路由专家数量是降低混合专家（MoE）模型推理服务成本的简便手段，但此前不清楚这样做究竟损害了什么能力、损害发生在哪里。作者希望精确定位这种“专家削减”带来的性能损失来源。

Method: 在四个MoE模型家族上，针对短答案问答任务，采用token级、层级和注意力路径干预实验。具体做法包括：将专家数量减半、逐位置分析答案似然贡献、测量首个token的注意力质量/范数/最大激活变化、恢复“超级专家”或“大规模激活”，并在真实提示上把约4%的token保留全专家、分别放置在任务接口处或随机放置进行对比。

Result: 专家减半时，三个家族的F1损失主要来自答案形式——模型仍能找到答案但不再简短作答。Qwen3-30B-A3B的损失集中在答案前的两个chat-template token上，DeepSeek-V2-Lite则集中在开头8-32个指令token处，按答案似然计分别约占一半和五分之一。削减使首个token的注意力质量、范数和最大激活变化均小于4%；恢复超级专家或大规模激活最多挽回约四分之一损失，远低于保留敏感位置全专家。在约4%token为全专家的真实提示中，将其放在任务接口处可使F1与未修改模型相差1分以内，而随机放置于文档中则差约3分。首token时间在11K长度提示上下降约14%，2K时无明显变化。

Conclusion: MoE预填充阶段削减专家的损害是高度位置特异的，集中在提示词末端（答案边界）和起始处（指令区）。保留这些敏感位置的全专家路由是最有效的缓解策略，但由于所有敏感布局都位于提示词两端，尚无法区分“保留两端”规则与“识别应用标记”规则。收益随提示长度增加而显现。

Abstract: Running prompt tokens through fewer routed experts during prefill is a simple way to make mixture-of-experts (MoE) serving cheaper, yet it is unclear what this damages. We locate the damage with token-, layer- and attention-path interventions on short-answer question answering in four MoE families. At half the experts the F1 loss of three families is mostly answer form: the model still finds the answer but stops answering briefly. In Qwen3-30B-A3B this loss is made at the two chat-template tokens before the answer, and in DeepSeek-V2-Lite at the first 8-32 prompt tokens, where its instruction sits; by answer likelihood these positions account for about half and a fifth of the damage. Reduction changes the first token's attention mass, norm and largest activation by less than 4%, and restoring super experts or massive activations recovers at most about a quarter of the loss, well below keeping the sensitive positions full. On real prompts with about 4% of tokens at full experts, placing them on the task interface keeps F1 within one point of the unmodified model, while placing them at random in the documents is about 3 points worse (two of five prespecified primary comparisons; the others were undecided or not run). Because the answer boundary always ends a single-turn prompt, and DeepSeek's loss was no longer detectable once its instruction moved away from the start, the sensitive tokens lay at the prompt ends in every sensitive layout we found, so rules that keep the ends are not distinguished from rules that read application marks. Time to first token falls about 14% on 11K-token prompts and not at 2K tokens.

</details>


### [110] [From Parallels to Divergence: Language Models' Learning of Action Nominal Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22468)
*Iskar Deng,Shane Steinert-Threlkeld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文用语法工程方法构造96种合成语言，训练GPT-2-small考察动作名词化构式（ANC）的可学习性，发现模型偏好与类型学分布在基本小句配置上一致，但在补语系统和ANC策略上存在偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作名词化构式的论元标记既可能遵循小句模式也可能遵循普通名词短语模式，其跨语言分布成因不清；作者希望检验模型的学习偏好能否解释自然语言的类型学分布。

Method: 将自然语料的共享语义输入与计算语法结合，构造在小句语序、名词短语语序、配列、补语系统和ANC策略五个维度上变化的96种目标语言；每种语言从头训练GPT-2-small，并用覆盖34种语法现象的定向最小对立对进行评估。

Result: 基本小句配置上呈现与类型学一致的学习偏好；补语系统与ANC策略上出现偏离，且模型会学到自然语言中罕见甚至未见的组合。

Conclusion: 模型学习与类型学分布只是部分对齐，说明自然语言分布既反映一般学习压力，也反映模型实验未能充分捕捉的功能需求。

Abstract: Action nominal constructions (ANCs) express events in nominal form, with their argument marking following patterns found in clauses or ordinary noun phrases. In this paper, we investigate how different grammatical configurations affect language models' learning of ANCs and related constructions, and how these learning preferences compare with typological distributions in natural languages. We use a grammar-engineered method for synthetic corpus construction that combines shared semantic input from natural corpora with computational grammars. We construct 96 target languages varying in clause word order, noun-phrase word order, alignment, complement system, and ANC strategy. For each language, we train GPT-2-small models from scratch and evaluate them using targeted minimal pairs covering 34 grammatical phenomena. The results show typological parallels in basic clause configurations but divergences in complement systems and ANC strategies, with models also learning combinations rare or unattested in natural languages. This partial alignment suggests that natural language distributions may reflect both general learning pressures and functional demands not fully captured by model learning experiments.

</details>


### [111] [RealCLI: Toward a Unified Evaluation of Human-Agent Collaboration for CLI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22507)
*Yunqi Liu,Peiyu Wang,Bingli Wang,Leo Luo,Xuanwu Song,Xudong Ma,Hongxin Li,Yaxin Du,Weicheng Gu,Fanglin Xu,Yi Xing,XiangLong Liu,Jujie He,Zhange Zhang,Huatong Song,Zhaoyi Li,Deyang Kong,Chuan Hao,Peihao Wu,Mingjie Tang,Ran Tao,Jian Yang,Wayne Xin Zhao,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 REALCLI，一个配对基准，用于在真实命令行任务上统一评估任务执行效果与人机协作质量；由五维框架（22 子维度、51 个交互场景模板）构成，并设 EXEC（一次性给出完整规格）与 COLLAB（脚本化用户渐进补充信息、纠错、修改约束与授权）两种模式，揭示前沿智能体在任务有效性与协作质量上的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CLI 智能体基准存在两类局限：一是默认用户指令在整个执行过程中保持不变，不符合长周期任务的真实情况；二是只评估任务最终结果，未刻画运行过程中的人机协作质量。因此缺乏能同时衡量"任务能否完成"与"协作过程是否良好"的评测手段。

Method: 构建配对基准 REALCLI：设计五维协作质量评估框架，细化为 22 个子维度和 51 个交互场景模板；提供两种配对对照模式——REALCLI-EXEC 在任务开始时给出完整规格，REALCLI-COLLAB 引入脚本化用户，在执行中逐步提供信息、纠错、修订约束并做出授权决策，从而对同一任务在两种交互条件下的表现进行对比。

Result: 评估显示前沿智能体存在明显能力缺口：最佳配置平均得分仅 49.2%；在以联合协作得分 J_collab 衡量时，最高性能仅为 37.6%。任务有效性与协作质量出现明显背离，模型在可引导性（steerability）、信任校准（trust calibration）和安全边界管理方面持续表现不佳。

Conclusion: REALCLI 提供了一个严谨、可复现的评测基础，用于测量并推动面向协作的 CLI 智能体发展；同时表明仅看任务完成率不足以反映真实部署中的协作能力，未来智能体需在动态指令、约束变更与授权边界下提升可引导性与安全可靠性。

Abstract: Command-line interface (CLI) agents increasingly execute longer-horizon tasks, yet existing benchmarks either assume that user instructions remain fixed throughout execution or evaluate task outcomes without capturing runtime collaboration quality. In this paper, we present REALCLI, a paired benchmark that unifies the evaluation of task execution and human-agent collaboration quality on realistic CLI tasks. To comprehensively measure collaboration, REALCLI builds a five-dimensional framework, operationalized into 22 sub-dimensions and 51 interaction scenario templates. REALCLI provides two modes for paired comparison: REALCLI-EXEC supplies complete specifications upfront, whereas REALCLI-COLLAB introduces a scripted user that delivers progressive information, corrections, constraint revisions, and authorization decisions. Evaluations reveal profound capability gaps in frontier agents: the best configuration attains an average score of only 49.2%, and when evaluated by the joint collaboration score ($J_{\mathrm{collab}}$), the peak performance reaches merely 37.6%. These results show that task effectiveness diverges sharply from collaboration quality, with models consistently struggling in steerability, trust calibration, and safety boundary management. REALCLI provides a rigorous, reproducible foundation for measuring and advancing collaborative CLI agents.

</details>


### [112] [AuditKIT: A Unified Evaluation Spine for Benchmarking, Judging, and Comparing Large Language Models and Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22511)
*Shreeyans Arora,Utsav Avaiya,Zera Lyngkhoi,Hem Gosalia,Ram Mohan Rao Kadiyala,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu,Pratinav Seth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 AuditKIT：一个开源（source-available）的评测库，用统一的结果表示记录基准准确率、判官评估、运行时性能、工具调用行为与检索质量等多类测量，并通过方向性指标契约、配置指纹、可扩展指标与后端，解决接口/指标语义/轨迹格式不一致导致的比较困难与失败被掩盖的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评测语言模型与智能体需要综合基准准确率、judge 评估、运行时性能、工具使用行为和检索质量；但接口、指标语义与轨迹格式的差异使这些比较复杂化，并可能掩盖 agentic 工作流中的失败。

Method: AuditKIT 以单一结果表示统一记录各类测量，提供方向感知的指标契约、配置指纹、可扩展的指标与后端；针对智能体提供按轮次的工具调用匹配、区分并行与串行执行、将已验证任务结果与轨迹级诊断分离，缺失或不可观测证据标记为 unscored 而非计为失败；针对 RAG 结合排序检索指标、judge 式接地性评估与确定性证据校验。

Result: 通过覆盖模型比较、服务运行时、检索、工具调用与互操作性的七个案例研究，展示了答案抽取、运行时行为和指标选择如何改变评测结论。

Conclusion: 评测结论对答案抽取、运行时行为与指标选择高度敏感，因此需要统一的、可审计的评测基础设施；AuditKIT 提供了这样的统一框架与可扩展机制。

Abstract: Evaluating language models and agents requires combining benchmark accuracy, judge-based assessment, runtime performance, tool-use behavior, and retrieval quality. Differences in interfaces, metric semantics, and trace formats complicate these comparisons and can hide failures in agentic workflows. We present AuditKIT, a source-available evaluation library that records these measurements in one result representation, with direction-aware metric contracts, configuration fingerprints, and extensible metrics and backends. For agents, it provides turn-aware tool-call matching, separates parallel from sequential execution, and distinguishes verified task outcomes from trajectory-level diagnostics. Missing or unobservable evidence is marked unscored rather than counted as failure. For retrieval-augmented generation, it combines ranked retrieval metrics, judged grounding, and deterministic evidence checks. Seven case studies spanning model comparison, serving runtimes, retrieval, tool calling, and interoperability show how answer extraction, runtime behavior, and metric selection change evaluation outcomes.

</details>


### [113] [alkhder at ImageEval 2026 Shared Tasks: Constrained Selection and Rotation-Averaged Logit Scoring for Multimodal Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22515)
*Maher Pasa,Maria Hanini,Amro Najjar,Hasan Alkhder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了 ImageEval 2026 Subtask 1b 的参赛系统：用预训练 7B 视觉语言模型，以零样本方式从三个候选陈述中为图像选出唯一视觉上成立的陈述（英、阿双语），借助选项拉丁方轮转抑制位置偏差，并以 next-token 对数概率作答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该子任务要求在多语（英语与标准阿拉伯语）条件下判断哪条陈述与图像视觉上一致，属于细粒度视觉—语言匹配问题；作者希望在不做任务特定训练的条件下检验现成大模型零样本能力，并解决多选设置中普遍存在的答案位置偏差。

Method: 采用预训练 7B 视觉语言模型，零样本、不进行任务特定训练或微调；将任务形式化为受限选择问题，利用模型对每个候选的 next-token 对数概率进行打分并选取得分最高者；为降低答案位置偏差，对候选陈述施加拉丁方轮转。

Result: 在英文测试集上 CI 得分为 0.0510，在标准阿拉伯语测试集上为 0.0960；相较官方在 devtest 上报告的 3B 基线，CI 分别降低 83.7% 与 80.4%（CI 越低越好）。

Conclusion: 简单的零样本概率打分配合位置偏差缓解策略，即可在双语视觉陈述选择任务上显著优于 3B 基线，说明预训练 7B 视觉语言模型具备较强的跨语言视觉判断能力，且无需额外微调。

Abstract: We describe our submission to Subtask~1b of ImageEval 2026. The task consists of selecting the single visually valid statement from three candidates paired with an image, and is in both English and Modern Standard Arabic (MSA). Our system uses a pre-trained 7B vision--language model in a zero-shot setting, without task-specific training or fine-tuning. We formulate the task as a constrained selection problem and choose the answer using next-token log-probabilities. To reduce answer-position bias, we apply a Latin-square rotation to the candidate statements. Our system achieves CI scores of 0.0510 on the English test set and 0.0960 on the MSA test set, reducing CI relative to the official 3B baselines reported on devtest by 83.7\% and 80.4\%, respectively.

</details>


### [114] [Learning User Representations to Improve Simulation Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22518)
*Shuhaib Mehri,Philippe Laban,Michel Galley,Dilek Tür,Jennifer Neville,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出用户表示（natural-language descriptions）是用户模拟器的被忽视瓶颈，提出对话重构任务以评估用户表示，并基于事后反馈蒸馏训练出 UserRepGen-4B 生成器，其表示比 GPT-5.5 更能实现忠实模拟并保留真实用户群体的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用户模拟器难以复现真实用户的多样行为，导致 AI 助手对代表性不足的用户群体泛化差。作者将"用户表示"——即以自然语言描述来条件化模拟器复现特定用户行为——识别为一项研究不足的关键瓶颈，且表示质量只能通过其诱导出的模拟行为事后判断。

Method: 提出对话重构任务：给定原始对话，生成用户表示→以其条件化模拟器→模拟对话→比较原始与模拟对话中用户行为以衡量忠实度。该比较同时产生事后反馈（指出表示缺陷与改进方向）。在此基础上提出 hindsight distillation：一种在线训练过程，将反馈条件下的改进蒸馏回表示生成器，使其能直接从原始对话生成更优表示。据此训练 UserRepGen-4B。

Result: 在覆盖七个数据集的 700 段对话上，事后反馈可在测试时改进用户表示；UserRepGen-4B 生成的表示使模拟忠实度超过 GPT-5.5 生成的表示。此外，学到的表示在未见任务上提升模拟忠实度，更好地保留真实用户群体层面的多样性，并更好地区分跨对话的不同用户。

Conclusion: 用户表示是用户模拟的关键瓶颈，可通过"重构—反馈—蒸馏"闭环被自动评估与优化；4B 规模的专用表示生成器即可超越大型通用模型，并在泛化、群体多样性与用户区分度上带来增益。

Abstract: User simulators enable interactive training and evaluation of AI assistants at scale. Yet current simulators struggle to reproduce the diverse behaviors of real users, leading to assistants that generalize poorly to underrepresented users. We identify user representations as an underexplored bottleneck in user simulation. User representations are natural-language descriptions that condition simulators to reproduce a particular user's behavior. A representation's quality becomes apparent in hindsight, through the behavior it induces in a simulator. To evaluate and optimize user representations, we formulate a conversation reconstruction task: given an original conversation, we generate a representation, condition a simulator on it, simulate a conversation, and then measure fidelity by comparing user behavior in the original and simulated conversations. This comparison also yields hindsight feedback, which identifies where the representation falls short and how it can be improved. We introduce hindsight distillation, an online training procedure that distills feedback-conditioned improvements into the representation generator, enabling it to generate improved representations directly from the original conversation. We present UserRepGen-4B, a representation generator trained with hindsight distillation. Across 700 conversations spanning seven datasets, hindsight feedback helps improve user representations at test time, and UserRepGen-4B generates representations that enable more faithful simulation than those generated by GPT-5.5. Beyond conversation reconstruction, learned representations improve simulation fidelity for unseen tasks, better preserve the population-level diversity of real users, and better distinguish users across conversations.

</details>


### [115] [alkhder at AlexandriaX-2026: Context-Aware English-to-Dialectal Arabic Dialogue Translation with QLoRA-Fine-Tuned NileChat-3B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22525)
*Hasan Alkhder,Maher Pasa,Maria Hanini,Amro Najjar,Ahmet Zengin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 团队使用QLoRA微调NileChat-3B-Base，在Alexandria平行对话语料上训练一套覆盖13种阿拉伯方言的Adapter，实现英阿方言对话翻译，私测集spBLEU 20.36、chrF++ 36.64。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlexandriaX-2026共享任务子任务1要求将英语对话轮次翻译为13种阿拉伯方言，方言多样性与对话上下文依赖带来挑战，且需在受限赛道（仅用Alexandria数据、算力有限）下完成。

Method: 以QLoRA微调NileChat-3B-Base，13种方言共用一套Adapter权重；每个对话轮次的翻译以目标国家与方言、领域、参与者角色、说话人—受话人性别方向及前文对话上下文为条件；推理时自回归翻译对话，将模型自身先前输出回灌为上下文，不使用任何测试集金标准目标侧信息。

Result: 受限赛道下训练约1小时，私测集翻译耗时不足2小时；两条赛道均取得国家宏平均spBLEU 20.36、chrF++ 36.64。

Conclusion: 单模型、单Adapter的多方言条件化微调方案在资源受限条件下可高效完成多方言对话翻译，性能稳定且推理成本低，为方言对话机器翻译提供了可行基线。

Abstract: This paper describes team Alkhder's submission to Subtask 1 of the AlexandriaX-2026 shared task, which asks systems to translate English dialogue turns into thirteen Arabic dialects. We fine-tune NileChat-3B-Base with QLoRA on the Alexandria parallel dialogue corpus, training one set of adapter weights for all dialects. Each turn is translated conditioned on the target country and dialect, the domain, the participant roles, the speaker-addressee gender direction, and the preceding dialogue context. At inference, conversations are translated autoregressively and the model is fed its own previous outputs back as context without using any gold target-side test information. Under the constrained track, using only Alexandria data, it trains in about one hour and translates the private test set in under two hours. The system reaches a country-macro-averaged spBLEU of 20.36 and chrF++ of 36.64 on both tracks.

</details>


### [116] [Literal Lock-In: How Test Language Shifts Literal Attraction in Typed Decision Models on Bengali Idioms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22526)
*Adib Sakhawat,Syed Rifat Raiyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文审计三类决策模型（编码器打分器、解码器打分器、闭源API），通过在输入选项列表中加入成语自身的字面义项进行干预，发现三者均被"字面锁死"：额外字面选项吸收了各臂剩余孟加拉语错误的65%–96%，且同一批配对条目上孟加拉语被捕获程度是英语的1.9–6.4倍；校准全面退化，开放模型置信度反转，无意义选项列表上仍报出0.82的平均置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型（仅对调用方给定选项输出概率、不生成文本）正被广泛用作路由器、分类器和防护栏，但因其不生成任何内容，原理阐释与思维链式审计方法都不适用，这类模型缺乏可用的审计手段。作者希望仅通过对输入的干预来检验这类模型的可靠性与校准，并关注其在不同测试语言（孟加拉语 vs 英语）下是否表现出系统性脆弱。

Method: 选取专家标注的孟加拉语成语，每条成语带有一个标准比喻义项和一个标准字面义项。审计三个系统：一个编码器打分器、一个解码器打分器和一个闭源API。全程仅对输入做干预：将成语自身的字面义项插入候选选项列表，观察选择是否被吸引。构建"模型×语言"矩阵，在共享的同一组条目上比较孟加拉语与英语的配对表现；同时测量三者在被干扰条件下的校准变化，并测试无语义选项列表（准确率处于随机水平）下的置信度表现。

Result: 三个系统全部被字面义项吸引，该干扰项吸收了每个系统剩余孟加拉语错误的65%至96%。同一批配对条目上，各系统在孟加拉语中的被捕获率是英语中的1.9至6.4倍；商业模型表现出的"抵抗力"实际上是其测试语言的属性；模型能力并不决定该失效的严重程度。三个系统的校准均退化；两个开放模型出现置信度反转，导致按置信度门控的效果还不如随机门控；在准确率仅为随机的无语义选项列表上，校准最好的系统仍报出0.82的平均置信度。

Conclusion: 不生成文本的决策模型同样存在可被输入干预诱发的系统性失效，且该失效与测试语言强烈相关、与模型能力无关，置信度不可作为门控依据。审计这类模型时应采用配对的多语言输入干预设计，而非依赖自身输出或置信度信号。

Abstract: Decision models, non-generative scorers that report a probability over caller-supplied options and emit no text, are increasingly deployed as routers, classifiers and guardrails. Because they generate nothing, rationales and chain-of-thought do not apply. We audit three: an encoder scorer, a decoder scorer and a closed API, entirely through interventions on their input, using expert-annotated Bengali idioms carrying a gold figurative and a gold literal gloss. Dropping an idiom's own literal gloss into the option list draws every arm toward it, taking 65% to 96% of each arm's remaining Bengali errors. A model-by-language matrix over one shared item set shows how far this depends on the test language: every arm is 1.9 to 6.4 times more captured in Bengali than in English on the same paired entries, the commercial model's apparent resistance is a property of its test language, and capability does not order the failure. Calibration degrades in all three arms, and on the open arms confidence inverts, so gating on it is worse than gating at random. On a semantics-free option list, where accuracy is at chance, the best-calibrated arm still reports mean confidence 0.82. Code and data: https://github.com/sakhadib/literal-lock-in.

</details>


### [117] [Pearl-Chat: Engineering a native Luganda language model from first principles in JAX and Flax NNX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22529)
*Wesley Kambale,Roland Sankara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Pearl-Chat 是一个约 8.5M 参数、面向卢干达语的低资源语言模型，用字节级 BPE 分词器将词元膨胀率降到 1.60，并通过两阶段训练在高资源语言占比极低的情况下实现显著压缩与流畅对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流自回归基座模型偏向高资源印欧语系，通用多语分词器在黏着型班图语言上词元膨胀率常超 3.2 tokens/word，导致上下文效率下降与延迟升高，因此需要一个专为卢干达语设计的高效小模型。

Method: 基于 JAX 与 Flax NNX 从第一性原理构建 8,473,122 参数的因果解码器 Transformer，采用函数式状态分解（nnx.split/merge）、Google Grain 确定性可恢复数据流和 jax.sharding.Mesh 的 SPMD 张量并行；训练专门的字节级 BPE 分词器（词表 8,192），在两阶段课程下训练：预训练 59,501 条序列，再用 510 段对话进行仅助手损失掩码的指令微调。

Result: 分词器达到每 token 4.88 字符、每词 1.60 token 的压缩水平，较 LLaMA-3 (128K) 与 Gemma (256K) 提升约两倍压缩率；4,000 步优化中验证交叉熵从 9.4695（困惑度 12,958.98）单调降至 0.1818（困惑度 1.20）；在三级留出诊断基准上取得 0.9792 ROUGE-L 与 0.9405 BLEU，并保持班图语名词类的协调一致。

Conclusion: 针对低资源黏着语的专用小模型与定制分词器能显著提升计算效率与生成质量，作者开源了权重、分词器与代码并提供 Python SSE 流式运行时，为该类语言的可复现研究提供了参考。

Abstract: Autoregressive foundation language models predominantly target high-resource Indo-European languages, imposing computational and morphological penalties on under-represented African languages. Generic multilingual tokenizers routinely exceed fertility rates of 3.2 tokens per word on agglutinative Bantu languages, degrading context efficiency and inflating latency. We present Pearl-Chat, an 8,473,122-parameter ($\sim$8.5M) causal decoder-only transformer engineered for Luganda (Oluganda, ISO 639-2: lg), a low-resource Niger-Congo Bantu language spoken by over 10 million people in East Africa. Built from first mathematical principles in JAX and Flax NNX, Pearl-Chat uses explicit functional state decomposition (nnx.split and nnx.merge), deterministic resumable data streaming via Google Grain, and SPMD tensor parallelism using jax.sharding.Mesh. We introduce a byte-level byte-pair encoding (BPE) subword tokenizer trained from scratch on curated Luganda corpora ($V = 8,192$), achieving 4.88 characters per token compression and 1.60 tokens per word fertility, a two-fold compression improvement over LLaMA-3 (128K) and Gemma (256K). The model is trained with a two-stage curriculum: Stage 1 pre-training on 59,501 sequences across parallel, biblical, encyclopedic, and journalistic corpora, and Stage 2 instruction fine-tuning on 510 dialogues with assistant-only loss masking. Across 4,000 optimization steps, validation cross-entropy loss drops monotonically from 9.4695 (perplexity: 12,958.98) to 0.1818 (perplexity: 1.20), a decrease of 9.2877 nats. On a three-tier held-out diagnostic benchmark, Pearl-Chat achieves 0.9792 ROUGE-L and 0.9405 BLEU while maintaining concordial agreement across Bantu noun classes. We provide a Python Server-Sent Events (SSE) streaming runtime and open-source all weights, tokenizers, and code.

</details>


### [118] [HeRE: A Novel Benchmark for Hebrew Relation Extraction via LLM-Guided Denoising of Knowledge Graph Alignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22542)
*Ron Keinan,Amir David Nissan Cohen,Reut Tsarfaty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展、语言无关的关系抽取（RE）数据构建流程：通过对知识库与文本的自然对齐进行去噪，并用自然语言推理（NLI）判定"文本是否蕴含关系三元组"来定义正确对齐；在希伯来语上系统比较五种去噪策略，最终发布150万条规模的希伯来语银标准RE数据集HeRE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在英语RE上表现强，但低资源语言的RE评测缺乏标准化基准：人工母语标注成本高、需专家；依赖翻译则会得到次优、不准确且带英语中心偏置的数据集。

Method: 提出语言无关的可扩展流水线，利用知识库与对应文本中天然存在但含噪的对齐来生成RE数据；将正确对齐形式化为NLI任务（输入文本蕴含关系三元组），据此去噪，并实证评估五种去噪策略；在少样本LLM设定下比较多语言模型与语言特定模型，在NLI微调与无标签交叉训练设定下比较希伯来语专用编码器；同时比较母语提示与英语提示的效果。

Result: 少样本LLM设定下多语言模型优于语言特定模型；希伯来语专用编码器在NLI微调下具竞争力，在无标签交叉训练下最强。母语提示与英语提示效果相当或略优，说明流程在目标语言中自然运作。最佳开源模型Gemma-4-31B-it获得F1=0.915，与最佳API模型GPT-5.4（0.921）相当，在完全留出、双人标注的测试集上达F1=0.844。基于优化后的流程发布HeRE：150万实例的希伯来语RE银数据集。

Conclusion: HeRE弥补了希伯来语RE标准评测的关键空白，并为NLP社区提供了可复现、语言无关的RE数据集构建流程，可推广到其他低资源语言场景。

Abstract: While LLMs demonstrate strong capabilities in English Relation Extraction (RE) tasks, a significant gap remains in assessing their RE performance in low-resource languages, due to a lack of standardized benchmarks. Creating native, language-specific annotated RE benchmarks is expensive and requires human expertise, while relying on translation yields suboptimal, inaccurate datasets that introduce English-centric biases. To tackle this, we propose a scalable, language-agnostic methodology to create RE data by denoising naturally occurring yet noisy alignments of knowledge bases and corresponding texts. We define a proper alignment via a Natural Language Inference (NLI) task, where a relation triplet is entailed by an input text. We empirically assess five denoising strategies, and demonstrate that multilingual models outperform language-specific models in the few-shot LLM setting, while Hebrew-specific encoders remain competitive under NLI fine-tuning and are strongest in the label-free cross-training setting. We further find that prompting in the native language achieves performance on par with or slightly exceeding English prompts, confirming that the pipeline operates naturally in the target language. The best open-weights model, Gemma-4-31B-it (F1\,=\,0.915), matches the best API model, GPT-5.4 (0.921), and reaches F1\,=\,0.844 on a fully held-out, dual-annotated test set. Applying the optimized pipeline, we release HeRE: a novel, massive, 1.5-million-instance silver dataset for Hebrew RE, designed to support data-intensive experimentation and model fine-tuning. HeRE bridges a critical gap in standard Hebrew RE evaluation, while providing the NLP community with a reproducible, language-agnostic pipeline for RE dataset construction in other low-resource settings.

</details>


### [119] [Cultural (In)Competence: Investigating Culture-Specific Stereotype Detection Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22545)
*Maximilian Spliethöver,Jannes Lötfering,Dominik Bachmann,Alexander Espig,Willem Zuidema,Katrin Schulz,Henning Wachsmuth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型语言模型在文化特定刻板印象检测中的有效性，考察提示语言、指令微调语言和微调数据规模三个因素，覆盖美国、荷兰与中国三种文化语境。结果显示模型难以可靠地考虑文化语境且过度预测刻板印象的存在；非英语提示有助于改善对文化语境的考量；指令微调可降低假阳性率；训练数据规模对检测效果呈非单调影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻板印象是受文化语境中介的主观建构，建模刻板印象需要文化能力。LLM可用于刻板印象检测等任务，但其可获得的文化知识存在差异，因而在不同语言与文化语境下的刻板印象处理能力可能不均衡（如非英语语言或非美国语境）。作者因此希望系统厘清影响文化特定刻板印象检测效果的关键因素。

Method: 围绕三个因素开展系统性实验：(1) 提示语言（prompting language）；(2) 指令微调语言（instruction tuning language）；(3) 微调数据集规模。实验覆盖美国、荷兰和中国三种文化语境，比较LLM在文化特定刻板印象检测任务上的表现。

Result: LLM不能可靠地考虑文化语境，并倾向于过度预测刻板印象的存在（假阳性偏高）。非英语提示似乎能提升对文化语境的考虑，从而带来更有效的预测。额外的指令微调可降低假阳性率。训练数据规模对检测有效性的调节作用呈非单调关系。

Conclusion: 文化特定刻板印象检测的效果显著受提示语言、指令微调语言与数据规模影响，模型当前的文化能力仍不足。实践上应重视非英语提示与指令微调的作用，并注意数据规模并非越大越好，需针对目标文化语境进行适配与评估。

Abstract: Stereotypes are subjective constructs that are mediated by the cultural context in which they are viewed. Successfully modeling them thus also requires cultural competence. When using large language models (LLMs) for stereotype-related tasks, such as their detection, it is therefore crucial that models can access relevant cultural knowledge and adjust responses accordingly. Since LLMs have shown disparities in what cultural knowledge is available to them, their capacity regarding stereotypes may also be disparate across languages and cultural contexts (e.g., for non-English languages or for non-US contexts). In this paper, we systematically investigate the effectiveness of LLMs for culture-specific stereotype detection across three factors: (1) the prompting language, (2) the instruction tuning language, and (3) the fine-tuning dataset size. We conduct experiments for US, Dutch, and Chinese cultural contexts. Our results suggest that LLMs do not reliably account for cultural contexts and overpredict the presence of stereotypes. Non-English prompts seem to improve consideration of cultural context and thus lead to more effective predictions. Moreover, we find that additional instruction tuning reduces false-positive rates and that training data size moderates detection effectiveness non-monotonically.

</details>


### [120] [Who Do Perspectivist Models Predict Well? Annotator Agreement, Sparsity, and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22567)
*London Lowmanstone,Vaagishwaran Sivakumar,Anwesha Samaddar,Akul Mundada,Harnoor Kaur,Maria Gini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究构建了迄今最大的带序数标注的视角主义NLP数据集集合，并发现视角主义算法对"与他人类似"的标注者预测效果更好，而标注者的标注数量（响应率）与算法性能无显著正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视角主义NLP标注任务要求算法对齐多种视角，以预测不同人类如何评价文本。但现有研究尚不清楚：当某些标注者行为异于常人（标注偏离他人或标注量较少）时，算法性能如何变化。作者希望刻画标注者个体特性与算法性能之间的关系。

Method: 1）整理出目前最大的序数标注视角主义NLP数据集集合；2）在各数据集上评估多类视角主义算法；3）对单个标注者的"特异性"（与他人的分歧程度）与算法性能之间的关系进行建模分析，并检验响应率的影响。

Result: 总体而言，算法在倾向于与他人一致的标注者上表现更好；但标注者的响应率（标注数量）与算法性能之间不存在统计学上显著的正相关。

Conclusion: 视角主义算法的性能并非均匀分布于所有标注者，而是系统性偏向"主流一致型"标注者，这对标注资源分配与模型公平性评估具有启示意义；同时，标注量多少不足以解释性能差异。

Abstract: Successful completion of perspectivist natural language processing (NLP) annotation tasks requires algorithms to be aligned across diverse perspectives in order to predict how different humans will rate text. This work studies how the performance of such algorithms varies for annotators who behave differently than others, either by providing annotations that diverge from others' responses or by providing fewer annotations altogether. To aid with characterizing model performance, we curate the largest collection to date of perspectivist NLP datasets with ordinal annotations. After evaluating multiple classes of perspectivist algorithms across each of the datasets, we model the relationship between individual annotator idiosyncrasy and algorithm performance. In general, we find that algorithms perform better on annotators who tend to agree with others, but that annotator response rate does not have a statistically significant positive correlation with algorithm performance.

</details>


### [121] [Mitigating Demographic Disparities in Explanation-Human Alignment via Rationale Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22586)
*Suryansh Talukdar,Taha Sarwat,Jonathan Rusert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文研究仇恨言论检测中的"解释公平性"，提出度量不同人口群体间解释合理性最大差异的指标 Plausibility-EFG，并基于 HateXplain 数据集评估 BERT、RoBERTa 与对抗训练模型 ADV-RoBERTa（结合 Integrated Gradients 与 SHAP），发现对抗训练可同时提升分类性能、解释与人标注理由的一致性并缩小解释差异，且预测层公平与解释层公平并非同一目标，最后提出 Fair-REF 框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分类性能并不意味着模型对不同人口群体给出同等合理的解释；现有公平性研究多关注预测结果层面的偏差，而解释层面的群体间差异可能被忽视，因此需要将"解释公平性"作为仇恨言论审核中的重要目标加以度量和缓解。

Method: 基于 HateXplain 数据集，将模型生成的 token 归因与人工标注的 rationale 进行比对，提出 Plausibility-EFG 指标，用于度量各人口群体间解释合理性的最大差距；在 BERT、RoBERTa 及对抗训练的 ADV-RoBERTa 上，采用 Integrated Gradients 和 SHAP 两种归因方法进行评估，并在训练、验证、测试划分上检验一致性；进一步开展公平性—性能权衡分析，并提出 Fair-REF 框架以减少群体间解释差异。

Result: ADV-RoBERTa 在提升分类性能的同时，改善了归因与人工 rationale 的对齐度，并降低了群体间的解释差异；该结论在两种归因方法及训练/验证/测试划分上均保持一致。仅降低预测层偏差并不能消除解释层差异，说明预测公平与解释公平相关但相互独立。公平性—性能分析显示，更强的对抗正则可进一步降低 Plausibility-EFG，但可能导致 macro-F1 小幅下降。

Conclusion: 解释公平性是仇恨言论审核中值得重视的独立目标；对抗训练是一种能同时改善性能与解释公平性的有效手段，但需权衡少量性能损失。作者提出 Fair-REF 框架用于缓解模型解释的群体间差异，为后续研究提供基线。

Abstract: We study explanation fairness in hate speech detection. High classification performance does not always mean that a model produces equally plausible explanations for different demographic groups. Using the HateXplain dataset, we introduce Plausibility-EFG, a metric that measures the largest difference in explanation plausibility across demographic groups by comparing model-generated token attributions with human-annotated rationales. We evaluate BERT, RoBERTa, and ADV-RoBERTa, an adversarially trained model, using Integrated Gradients and SHAP. Our results show that ADV-RoBERTa improves classification performance and alignment between explanations and human rationales while reducing explanation disparities across demographic groups. These improvements are observed with both attribution methods and remain consistent across training, validation, and test splits. We also show that reducing prediction-level bias alone does not eliminate disparities in explanations, suggesting that predictive fairness and explanation fairness are related but distinct objectives. Finally, our fairness-performance analysis shows that stronger adversarial regularization can further reduce Plausibility-EFG, although it may cause a small decrease in macro-F1. Overall, this work presents explanation fairness as an important objective in hate speech moderation and introduces Fair-REF, a framework designed to reduce disparities in model explanations across demographic groups.

</details>


### [122] [SAG2: Embedding-Free Multi-Hop Retrieval with BM25 and JEV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22588)
*Yuchao Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SAG2，一种基于单一 SQLite、BM25 与 JEV 上下文证据判定的无嵌入多跳检索架构，在无需离线嵌入与复杂数据库服务器的前提下取得领先的检索召回表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强多跳检索应便于实际部署，避免昂贵的语料预处理和复杂的数据库基础设施。现有方案依赖密集索引、LLM 事件-实体抽取与专用数据库服务，准备成本高。

Method: SAG2 沿用 SAG 的查询时关联原则，但将其事件-实体索引与密集索引替换为对原始段落构建的词法索引（BM25），以 JEV 的上下文证据决策驱动检索；已选段落提供后续检索词项，新检索到的证据引导后续判断，从而把语义证据选择移至查询时，免去离线嵌入计算、LLM 抽取与专用数据库服务。

Result: 在 MuSiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA 各 1,000 题子集上，SAG2 的 Recall@5 与 Recall@10 均为对比方法中最高，Recall@10 分别为 86.62%、97.43%、99.00%；在 511,962 文档的 EnterpriseRAG-Bench 上达到 81.10% 文档 Recall@10。索引全部 11,656 条 MuSiQue 段落仅需 2.36 秒，生成 25.87 MiB 数据库，且无需任何模型调用。

Conclusion: 精确的多跳证据检索可由紧凑、快速准备的基础设施支撑，SAG2 为知识检索应用提供了实用的基础方案。

Abstract: Strong multi-hop retrieval should be practical to deploy without expensive corpus preparation or complex database infrastructure. We present SAG2, an embedding-free retrieval architecture built around a single SQLite database, BM25, and contextual evidence decisions from JEV. SAG2 follows the query-time association principle of SAG (SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation), while replacing its event-entity and dense indexes with a lexical index over original passages. Selected passages supply terms for further retrieval, and newly retrieved evidence guides subsequent decisions. This moves semantic evidence selection to query time, eliminating offline embedding computation, LLM-based event-entity extraction, and dedicated database servers. The resulting system combines lightweight preparation with strong retrieval accuracy: on 1,000-question subsets of MuSiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and HotpotQA, SAG2 achieves the highest Recall@5 and Recall@10 among the compared methods, with Recall@10 of 86.62%, 97.43%, and 99.00%. It also reaches 81.10% document Recall@10 over the 511,962-document EnterpriseRAG-Bench corpus. Indexing all 11,656 MuSiQue passages takes 2.36 seconds, produces a 25.87 MiB database, and requires no model calls. SAG2 demonstrates that accurate multi-hop evidence retrieval can be supported by compact, rapidly prepared infrastructure, making it a practical foundation for knowledge retrieval applications.

</details>


### [123] [The Model Knows Better: Defending Medical Multi-Agent Systems Against Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22594)
*Fardin Ahsan Sakib,Ziwei Zhu,Ozlem Uzuner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 医疗多智能体系统中，提示注入可导致高达61%的错误决策且协作并无稳定防护作用；作者提出一种免训练的"临床判断核验"防御，将患者发现与临床判断分离并逐一核验，使MedQA上的攻击成功率降至4-7%且保持无攻击情形下的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体已被广泛用于临床决策支持，但攻击者注入的文本（提示注入）可操纵模型做出错误决策；该攻击对多智能体系统的影响机制及防护手段尚不明确，而现有防御只能部分奏效，无法识别人工写作般自然的临床文本注入。

Method: 先在2个系统、2个模型家族、3个基准上系统评测提示注入的影响；据此提出免训练防御：仿照临床实践，把患者客观发现与临床判断（诊断、建议、指令）分离，仅以患者发现为证据逐条核验每个临床判断，只有被支持判断才能支撑最终决策。另观察到被注入影响的模型在被要求核对注入文本与患者发现时通常会拒绝该文本。

Result: 单段注入文本在多达61%的案例中成功；协作不能稳定抵御攻击；现有防御仅部分有效。所提防御在每个基准上均优于全部基线，MedQA攻击成功率降至4-7%，同时保持无攻击案例准确率，并在医学专用模型、防御感知攻击与自适应攻击下依然有效。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作本身不足以保证临床决策的鲁棒性，需在决策环节对临床判断的证据支持性进行核验；免训练的判断核验防御可显著提升医疗多智能体系统的抗注入能力并具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Medical multi-agent systems, in which LLM agents discuss a case, are increasingly studied for clinical decision support. Prompt injection, text that an attacker adds to a model's input, can manipulate LLMs into wrong decisions. How it affects these systems, and how to protect them, remains unclear. Across two systems, two model families and three benchmarks, a single injected passage succeeds in up to 61% of cases, and collaboration does not consistently protect against it. Existing defenses help only partly: they miss injections that look like ordinary clinical text. Yet when asked to check the injected text against the patient's findings, a model that followed the injection usually rejects it. We therefore propose a training-free defense that verifies the clinical judgments behind the system's decision. Following clinical practice, it separates the patient's findings from clinical judgments, such as diagnoses, recommendations and instructions, and checks each judgment against the findings alone, so the decision rests only on supported judgments. Our defense outperforms all baselines on every benchmark, lowering attack success on MedQA to 4-7% while maintaining accuracy on cases without an attack, and remains effective with a medical model and against defense-aware and adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [124] [Broad and Efficient PII Detection with Unified Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22597)
*Andy Hsu,Konstantin Berlin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个141M参数的编码器式PII检测器，使用77个公开数据集共112万条样本，按统一的12类结构化PII详细定义重新标注，在低误报率工作点上以更低成本取得领先召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PII检测需在大规模流量下兼顾高召回与低误报率；现有方法依赖复杂架构增加部署成本，且仅用少量合成数据微调导致覆盖不足；直接合并公开数据集又因分类体系与标注口径不一致而产生矛盾信号。

Method: 构建141M参数编码器检测器；汇集77个公开数据集共1.12M样本；将各类别映射到统一模式后，依据"广泛上下文检测"的细粒度定义对12类结构化PII重新标注，以获得一致训练与评测信号。

Result: 在AI4Privacy与Nemotron-PII基准的低FPR工作点上，相对更大基线在自身标签下14项对比中11项召回最高、原标签下9项最高；增益集中于多语种披露、URL中嵌入的IP地址等额外覆盖；吞吐量高2-10倍，峰值GPU显存少94-97%。

Conclusion: 统一的重新标注口径加轻量编码器架构，可在显著更低成本下实现领先的PII检测性能，说明数据质量与标注一致性有时比模型规模更关键。

Abstract: Personally Identifiable Information (PII) detection models must efficiently find identifiers across diverse contexts while maintaining a low false-positive rate (FPR) on high-volume traffic. Recent detectors increasingly rely on complex architectures that raise deployment cost, yet many are fine-tuned on only a few synthetic datasets that limit their coverage. Combining public datasets is challenging because their taxonomies differ, and even after categories are mapped to a common schema, labels can disagree on what counts as a detection, producing inconsistent training and evaluation signals. To address these problems, we present a practical 141M-parameter encoder-based detector trained on 1.12 million examples from 77 public datasets, relabeled under a detailed definition of broad contextual detection for 12 structured PII categories. Evaluated on the AI4Privacy and Nemotron-PII benchmarks at a low-FPR operating point, our model achieves the highest recall against larger baselines in 11 of 14 comparisons under our labels and 9 of 14 under the original labels. Its largest recall gains include useful additional coverage, such as multilingual disclosures and IP addresses embedded in URLs. It achieves this with 2-10x higher throughput and 94-97% less peak GPU memory than the baselines, delivering leading detection performance at substantially lower cost.

</details>


### [125] [When Does Uncertainty Measure RAG Hallucination Risk?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22615)
*Rajeel Ahmad Ansari,Tautik Agrahari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文研究不确定性作为RAG幻觉风险度量的适用条件：只有当检索证据对答案形成约束时，不确定性才能有效指示幻觉风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性被广泛用于衡量RAG中的幻觉风险，但其有效性可能依赖于检索证据是否对答案构成约束。当检索覆盖不足时，模型可依赖内部知识作答并给无支持的主张赋予高概率，导致不确定性信号失真。

Method: 在HotpotQA与SQuAD上对四个指令微调模型进行测试；按词数分箱匹配忠实与幻觉答案以控制长度混淆；比较长度分类器与困惑度的AUROC；通过改变检索深度（如仅取Top-1段落与加入第二段）观察检测能力变化；并提出结合不确定性与语义漂移的复合分数。

Result: 长度分类器接近随机水平，而困惑度在针对性无支持编辑上达到0.81–0.91 AUROC；仅用Top-1段落时检测能力减弱，加入第二段后恢复；复合分数改善了校准效果，且在RAGTruth QA上仍高于随机水平。

Conclusion: 结果支持"条件性"观点：不确定性是衡量RAG幻觉风险的有用指标，但其有效性取决于检索证据对答案的约束程度，需与语义漂移等信号结合使用。

Abstract: Uncertainty can measure hallucination risk in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), but only when retrieved evidence constrains the answer. When evidence is present, unsupported factual claims become less likely; when retrieval coverage is poor, the model can answer from internal knowledge and assign high probability to unsupported claims. We test this across four instruction-tuned models on HotpotQA and SQuAD. After matching faithful and hallucinated answers within word-count bins, length-only classifiers fall near chance, while perplexity reaches 0.81--0.91 AUROC on targeted unsupported edits. Varying retrieval depth shows why the score changes meaning: detection can weaken with only the top-ranked passage, then recover after adding a second passage. A combined uncertainty and semantic-drift score improves calibration, and measurements remain above chance on RAGTruth QA. Results support a conditional view of uncertainty as a useful measure of RAG hallucination risk.

</details>


### [126] [Trajectory-Guided Steering: Mitigating Spurious Feature Bias in Clinical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22618)
*Fardin Ahsan Sakib,Ziwei Zhu,Ozlem Uzuner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出轨迹引导干预方法TGS，仅依据模型逐层预测偏好的演化轨迹来判断是否纠正以及纠正方向，无需反事实数据与虚假特征先验，可在临床文本分类中降低对性别等虚假特征的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在临床分类中依赖虚假特征：仅替换临床记录中的一个性别词，就会使与性别无关的诊断在最多53.5%的病例中发生变化。现有推理时纠正方法要么需要围绕特定虚假特征构造反事实数据，要么表现不稳定，缺乏一种通用且可靠的纠正判据。

Method: 作者观察到模型在层间对答案的偏好如何演化（即预测轨迹）能指示每篇文档的纠正是否可能有效，据此提出Trajectory-Guided Steering（TGS）：仅从轨迹本身决定对每条预测是否纠正以及纠正方向，不需要反事实数据，也不需要知道虚假特征是什么。

Result: 在i2b2的16个临床分类任务上，TGS纠正了17.7%的错误，仅破坏1.3%的正确预测，在所有测试超参数设置下均为净正收益，而所有基线方法均为净负或接近零。纠正同时降低虚假特征依赖：受影响最大的疾病中性别替换不一致率显著下降（高血压53.5%→38.0%，糖尿病35.1%→21.5%）。若对所有文档统一施加纠正，正确正例的破坏率从0.1%升至6.4%，说明逐文档选择至关重要。TGS还可迁移到三个模型家族及另外三个临床与生物医学数据集。

Conclusion: 预测轨迹可作为无需虚假特征先验的逐样本纠正信号，TGS在修正错误、保持正确预测稳定性和降低虚假特征依赖之间取得良好平衡，并具有跨模型与跨数据集的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models rely on spurious features in clinical classification: swapping a single gender token in clinical notes alters gender-irrelevant diagnoses in up to 53.5% of cases. Existing inference-time corrections either need counterfactual data built around the specific spurious feature or behave unpredictably. We observe that how a model's preference between answers evolves across layers, its prediction trajectory, indicates for each document whether a correction is likely to help. We propose Trajectory-Guided Steering (TGS), which decides from the trajectory alone whether to correct each prediction and in which direction, with no counterfactual data and no knowledge of the spurious feature. On 16 clinical classification tasks from i2b2, TGS fixes 17.7% of errors while breaking only 1.3% of correct predictions and stays net-positive across all tested hyperparameter settings; all baselines are net-negative or near zero. The same corrections reduce reliance on the spurious feature: on the most affected diseases, gender-swap inconsistency falls sharply (hypertension 53.5% -> 38.0%, diabetes 35.1% -> 21.5%). Applying the correction to every document instead raises breaks on correct positive predictions from 0.1% to 6.4%, so per-document selection is essential. TGS also transfers to three model families and three other clinical and biomedical datasets.

</details>


### [127] [A Plausible Explanation Is Enough to Mislead Multimodal Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22631)
*Anglin Cai,Zhiqi Wang,Xiaozhi Yang,Yurun Lin,Dongwon Lee,Yuchen Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出视觉问答风格基准 JUPE，在固定答案的前提下变换解释文本，发现14个多模态大模型评审员会被"听起来合理"的普通解释误导；普通解释与思维链解释分别使错误答案被拒绝的比例下降12.7与13.8个百分点，作者称之为"合理性捕获"（plausibility capture）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型越来越多地被用作训练与评测流程中的评委，且常常连同解释一起评估答案。已有研究显示AI评委对文本因素敏感，但尚不清楚普通的、听起来合理的解释是否足以左右那些本质上需要视觉核验的判断。若确实如此，则基于"答案+解释"的评审会系统性高估模型性能。

Method: 构建 JUPE 基准：采用视觉问答（VQA）形式，保持答案不变、仅改变配套解释（如普通理由陈述、思维链式推理），在多个领域、多种解释生成器和多种评审协议下测试14个 MLLM 评委；并引入信号检测论，将判断变化分解为判别力（d′）与判定标准（criterion）两个成分，同时检验若干缓解措施的有效性。

Result: 普通但听起来合理的解释即可误导评委，其平均效应与刻意说服性论证相当；普通理由与思维链理由分别使错误答案被拒绝的比例下降12.7和13.8个百分点。信号检测分析显示判定标准显著向"接受"方向偏移，而平均判别力未见明显变化。该效应跨领域、跨解释生成器、跨评审协议普遍存在，所测试的缓解手段仅提供有限且依赖具体模型的保护。

Conclusion: MLLM 评委存在"合理性捕获"这一系统性失效：解释的说服力替代了对视觉证据的核验，且并未提升其辨别能力。因此在评委同时看到答案与解释的设定下，性能估计会被抬高。文中提醒评测与训练管线应警惕解释带来的偏差，并指出需要更有效的防护机制。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly serve as judges in training and evaluation pipelines, often assessing answers together with explanations. Although AI judges are sensitive to textual factors, it remains unclear whether ordinary plausible explanations can sway judgments requiring visual verification. We introduce JUPE, a visual-question-answering (VQA)-style benchmark that fixes answers while varying explanations.
  Across 14 MLLM judges, ordinary explanations that sound plausible mislead judges, with effects comparable on average to deliberately persuasive arguments. Plain justifications and chain-of-thought rationales reduce rejection of incorrect answers by 12.7 and 13.8 percentage points, respectively. We call this failure plausibility capture. Using signal detection theory, we find a significant mean shift in decision criterion toward acceptance, without a clear change in mean discriminability. Explanations sway judgments across domains, explanation generators, and judging protocols, while tested mitigations offer limited, model-dependent protection. Such shifts can inflate performance estimates when judges evaluate answers with explanations.

</details>


### [128] [ToMStories: Interpreting Theory-of-Mind in Synthetic Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22632)
*Melanie Subbiah,Chia-En Jacklyn Tsai,Kathleen McKeown*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ToMStories数据集（约6000个合成短叙事，配对选择题/开放题，考查情绪、恐惧、动机、情感创伤），评测13个开源模型发现：最大模型在选择题上与人类持平，开放题上不及人类；模型在解读与生成隐含心理状态时存在简化倾向，且合成数据可能高估模型的真实人际解读能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解他人常需从行为（而非直述）推断其感受与意图，作者想检验语言模型能否做出此类推断、以及通过何种方式做到。

Method: 构建Theory-of-Mind Stories (ToMStories)评测数据集：约6000个合成生成的短篇叙事片段，每个片段配一个关于角色情绪、恐惧、动机或情感创伤的选择题或开放题；心理状态以隐含（showing）、明示（telling）、或对话与描述相互冲突三种方式呈现；评测1B–72B共13个开源权重模型，并进行错误与生成分析。

Result: 最大模型在选择题上与人类表现相当，但在开放题上不及人类；分析显示模型在隐含心理状态的解读与生成两方面都采用了简化策略；合成数据能揭示模型如何执行解读，但很可能高估模型解读真实人类的能力。

Conclusion: 合成叙事数据是研究模型心理状态推断机制的有用工具，但用作能力基准时会高估模型的真实心智理论水平，需谨慎解读并辅以更真实的数据。

Abstract: Understanding people often requires inferring what they feel or want from how they behave rather than from what they state outright. We ask whether language models can make these inferences, and how they do so. We introduce Theory-of-Mind Stories (ToMStories), an evaluation dataset of ~6,000 short synthetically generated narrative vignettes, each paired with a multiple-choice or open-ended question about a character's emotion, fear, motivation, or emotional wound. These psychological states are conveyed implicitly (showing), explicitly (telling), or with conflict between dialogue and description. Evaluating thirteen open-weight models (1B-72B parameters), we find that the largest models match humans on multiple choice but not open-ended questions. Our analyses point to simplifications models make in both interpretation and generation of implicit psychological states, indicating that synthetic data can help us understand how models perform interpretation, but likely overestimates how well models interpret humans.

</details>


### [129] [Measuring Mechanistic Uncertainty in Large Language Models via Causal Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22636)
*Aneesh Komanduri,Majid Afshar,Timothy A. Miller,Yanjun Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"机制性不确定性"概念，用因果鲁棒边际（扰动模型内部机制使输出改变所需的最小干预强度）来量化不确定性，并借助Jacobian Lens概念引导向量实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在关键领域应用广泛，其幻觉问题使得可靠且可解释的不确定性量化成为必要；现有不确定性方法多基于输出层面（如概率、熵），缺乏对模型内部机制的刻画。

Method: 将不确定性刻画为支撑模型回答的内部机制的稳定性；定义因果鲁棒边际为偏离模型输出所需的最小内部干预量，并利用Jacobian Lens概念引导向量将其操作化。

Result: 在3个开源模型和5个问答数据集上，错误与不确定回答的边际更小，说明其对内部干预更敏感；该边际与基于输出的不确定性方法互为补充。在受控知识注入实验中，训练后正确学得的事实边际增大。

Conclusion: 因果鲁棒边际提供了具有机制基础的、可解释的不确定性信号，可作为传统输出层面不确定性度量的补充。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in critical domains, where their susceptibility to hallucinations makes reliable and interpretable uncertainty quantification essential. We introduce the concept of mechanistic uncertainty, which characterizes uncertainty through the stability of the internal mechanisms supporting a model's response. We propose the causal robustness margin, defined as the minimum internal intervention required to deviate the model's output, and operationalize it using Jacobian Lens (Jlens) concept steering vectors. Across three open-source models and five question-answering datasets, we show incorrect and uncertain responses exhibit smaller margins, indicating greater sensitivity to internal interventions, and the causal robustness margin complements conventional output-based uncertainty methods. Finally, in a controlled knowledge injection experiment, correctly learned facts show increased margins after training, suggesting that causal robustness provides a mechanistically grounded signal of uncertainty.

</details>


### [130] [Inductive Learning of Concept Embeddings from Cross-Linguistic Colexification Patterns with Textual Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22638)
*Arne Rubehn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种归纳式概念嵌入学习框架，结合完整与部分共词化网络的结构信息以及简短概念描述的语义信息，使未见概念也能基于简短定义获得嵌入表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念嵌入依赖转导式方法，只能为训练数据中出现的节点学习嵌入，缺乏可扩展性；而历史与类型学语言比较仍多依赖离散表示，嵌入方法的潜力尚未被充分挖掘。

Method: 构建归纳式框架：利用完整与部分共词化网络的结构属性，并融合简短概念描述的语义信息，从而支持对训练中未出现概念进行嵌入。

Result: 所得概念嵌入与多种跨语言相似性或相关性度量相关，并且能较好地泛化到训练数据中未出现的新概念。

Conclusion: 该归纳框架克服了转导式概念嵌入的可扩展性瓶颈，为基于嵌入的跨语言语义比较提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The advent of distributional semantics as a framework for computational semantics marked a paradigm shift in the nature of representations, by which meanings are represented as dense vectors commonly known as word embeddings. Computational approaches to historical or typological language comparison, on the other hand, still mostly rely on discrete representations, while the potential of embedding-based methods remains largely untapped in this context. Recent studies have proposed concept embeddings as a language-independent, onomasiological counterpart to word embeddings, based on networks of cross-linguistic colexification patterns. While the resulting concept embeddings reflect meaningful semantic properties and relations, they suffer from a lack of scalability, since they rely on transductive embedding approaches which only learn embeddings for nodes present in the training data. In this study, we present an inductive framework for learning concept embeddings, combining the structural properties of full and partial colexification networks with semantic information from short concept descriptions. This framework allows the embedding of unseen concepts based on a short definition. We show that the resulting concept embeddings correlate with various cross-linguistic measures of similarity or relatedness, while generalizing well to new concepts that were absent from the training data.

</details>


### [131] [SAFETYEDIT: Sparse Internal Targets with Prompt-Wide Control for LLM Jailbreak Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22640)
*Yutong Ke,Yansu Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Xihao Guo,Chongwen Zhao,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SAFETYEDIT，以单个与拒绝相关的 MLP 坐标为稀疏内部目标，并借助早期注意力层的 KV-cache 标记将触发前缀信号传播到后续指令位置，在四个开源权重模型上取得了更高的触发攻击成功率与更强的有害回答质量，同时基本保持无触发行为与通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型编辑后门多以肯定式回复前缀为优化目标，一旦该前缀级目标被满足，模型仍可能回退到拒绝，或生成看似合规却不回应请求的回答；而基于内部表征的方法转向潜空间中的高维稠密"合规方向"，依赖保持性约束来维持条件性。作者由此提问：能否用更小的内部目标换取不同的"有效性—保持性"权衡，并受单坐标干预具备可比控制力的启发，选择稀疏的拒绝相关 MLP 坐标作为目标。

Method: SAFETYEDIT 优化单个拒绝相关坐标，并在早期注意力层使用 KV-cache 标记，把前缀触发信号传播到后续各指令位置；在部分检查点上由下游 MLP 读取器将该信号转化为对目标坐标的控制，在其他检查点上传播信号本身即可奏效。方法的定义取决于稀疏目标，而非固定的写入器—读取器组合。

Result: 在四个开源权重 LLM 与三个数据集组上，相对于各模型所评估的基线，SAFETYEDIT 在 12 组比较中的 11 组取得最高的触发式 Guard ASR，并在全部 12 组中取得最高的平均 StrongREJECT 分数。消融与行为审计表明，在测试配置下无触发行为与通用能力的变化总体很小。

Conclusion: 以单个稀疏拒绝相关坐标为内部目标、配合 KV-cache 前缀信号传播，可在提升触发有效性的同时较好地保持条件性与通用能力，说明稀疏内部目标能够提供一种有竞争力的有效性—保持性权衡。

Abstract: Model-editing backdoors aim to induce harmful responses under a trigger while preserving ordinary behavior on untriggered input. Recent model-editing approaches target affirmative response prefixes through rank-one updates to a small subset of model weights. Yet when this prefix-level target is satisfied, the model may still revert to refusal or produce a coherent, apparently compliant response that does not address the request. To overcome this limitation, existing internal-representation approaches shift focus from output tokens to dense, high-dimensional compliance directions in latent space. Existing representation-level approaches rely on preservation constraints to maintain conditionality, motivating us to ask whether a smaller internal target can offer a different effectiveness-preservation trade-off. Motivated by the comparable control capability of single-coordinate interventions, we investigate a sparse refusal-related MLP coordinate as this target. We propose SAFETYEDIT, which optimizes a single refusal-related coordinate and uses KV-cache marking in early attention layers to propagate a prefix-trigger signal across subsequent instruction positions. On some checkpoints a downstream MLP reader converts this signal into control of the coordinate, while on others the propagated signal alone suffices; the method is defined by the sparse target rather than by a fixed writer-reader pair. Across four open-weight LLMs and three dataset groups, SAFETYEDIT achieves the highest triggered Guard ASR in 11 of 12 comparisons and the highest mean StrongREJECT score in all 12, relative to the baselines evaluated for each model. Crucially, comprehensive ablation and behavioral audits confirm generally minimal changes in untriggered behavior and general capabilities in the tested configurations.

</details>


### [132] [Evidence-Gated Bilingual RAG for Reliable Consumer Banking Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22652)
*Ahmed Mohamed Mahmoud Rayyan,Rana Mahmoud Mohammed,Aya Abdallah Abdelmonem,Mohamed Khaled Goda Othman,Rahatara Ferdousi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个完全本地部署、具备证据门控（evidence-gated）的双语（阿拉伯语/英语）RAG 框架，用于消费者银行产品搜索，在 210 条查询上将 Top-1 命中率从 50.48% 提升到 79.05%，MRR@5 从 0.571 提升到 0.797。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费者常用自然语言描述需求（如"a card for travel"），与银行网站上产品页面所用的术语不一致，导致关键词匹配失效；双语服务中同一意图还可分别用阿拉伯语或英语表达，进一步加大检索难度。因此需要一种能弥合语言与措辞差异、并在本地基础设施上运行的银行搜索方案。

Method: 构建 evidence-gated 双语 RAG 框架，全部在本地基础设施运行。流程包括：结构化网页内容精炼、稠密+词汇混合检索（hybrid dense-lexical retrieval）、意图感知的证据选择、cross-encoder 重排序，以及"Answer / Related Mismatch / No Answer"三结果决策机制。

Result: 在 210 条带 URL 标注的阿拉伯语与英语查询上，相比混合检索基线，Top-1 命中率由 50.48% 提升至 79.05%，MRR@5 由 0.571 提升至 0.797。另设 26 例端到端评测，严格成功率 69.2%，其中 72.7% 的生成答案被自动化忠实性评判器判定为有证据支持。

Conclusion: 结果属初步性质：检索查询仅是小规模参考集的改写，端到端测试样本量小且使用自动而非人工评判，因此尚不能确立经人工核验的答案正确性或可泛化性能。

Abstract: Consumers often describe what they need rather than use the terminology found on a banking website. For example, a natural-language request ("a card for travel") may not share the keywords of the relevant product page, and the problem is harder in bilingual services, where the same intent may be expressed in Arabic or English. This paper presents an evidence-gated bilingual retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework for consumer banking search that operates entirely on local infrastructure. The framework combines structured web-content refinement, hybrid dense-lexical retrieval, intent-aware evidence selection, cross-encoder reranking, and a three-outcome Answer / Related Mismatch / No Answer decision mechanism. On 210 URL-labeled Arabic and English queries, the proposed approach improves Top-1 hit rate from 50.48% to 79.05% and MRR@5 from 0.571 to 0.797 over a hybrid baseline. A separate 26-case end-to-end evaluation achieved 69.2% strict success, with 72.7% of generated answers classified as supported by an automated faithfulness judge. These results are preliminary: the retrieval queries are paraphrases of a small reference set, and the end-to-end test is small and uses an automated rather than human judge, so they do not establish human-verified answer correctness or generalizable performance.

</details>


### [133] [Secondhand Stress: How Stressful Disclosures Affect Psychosocial Wellbeing in Online Graduate Admissions Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22657)
*Veda Duddu,Agam Goyal,Sabelle Huang,Ayush Umashankar Prasad,Mingquan Yi,Koustuv Saha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究基于压力交叉理论，分析两个Reddit社区三个招生周期的171,981条帖子和1,130,413条评论，发现申请者在备考期接触同伴的痛苦披露后，其焦虑、抑郁和压力表达上升，评论活动与词汇冗余度也增加；但下游与这些已暴露用户互动的人未检测到显著的心理社会关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社区为经历压力性生活转折（如研究生申请）的人提供自我披露和同伴支持的场所，但接触他人痛苦也可能对成员产生心理社会影响，而现有研究尚未充分厘清这种接触与后续表达、参与行为的关系及其是否沿互动链条传递。

Method: 以压力交叉理论为框架，采集两个Reddit社区三个招生周期的171,981条帖子和1,130,413条评论；采用倾向得分分层与基线校正的对比方法，考察接触同伴痛苦披露与后续心理社会表达及参与行为的关联，并检验下游互动者中是否存在类似关联。

Result: 在申请准备阶段接触痛苦披露的用户，随后表现出更高的分类器估计焦虑、抑郁和压力表达水平，同时评论活动更频繁、词汇冗余度更高；这些关联在多个招生周期中重复出现；而与已暴露用户互动的下游回应者中未检测到显著的心理社会关联。

Conclusion: 更强的痛苦相关表达可以与持续的社区参与并存，且直接接触痛苦披露与后续社交接触应加以区分；研究为设计既能保留自我披露与同伴支持机会、又能关注接触痛苦内容者体验的在线社区提供了启示。

Abstract: Online communities provide spaces for individuals navigating stressful life transitions to disclose difficulties and seek peer support. Yet encountering others' distress may also have psychosocial implications for community members. Drawing on stress crossover theory, we examine how engagement with peer distress during graduate admissions is associated with subsequent psychosocial expression and participation, and whether similar associations extend through downstream interactions. We analyze 171,981 posts and 1,130,413 comments from two Reddit communities across three admissions cycles, using propensity-score stratification and baseline-adjusted comparisons. Users who engaged with distress disclosures during application preparation subsequently exhibited higher classifier-estimated anxiety, depression, and stress expression, alongside greater commenting activity and lexical redundancy. These associations recurred across admissions cycles, while no significant psychosocial associations were detected among downstream respondents interacting with previously exposed users. Our findings highlight how greater distress-related expression can coexist with continued community participation, while distinguishing direct engagement with distress disclosures from subsequent social contact. We discuss implications for designing online communities that preserve opportunities for self-disclosure and peer support while remaining attentive to the experiences of those encountering distress-related content.

</details>


### [134] [ProPerAgent: Toward Memory-Driven Proactive Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22671)
*Shenglai Zeng,Xiao Yang,Tiffany Ma,Xinyuan Zhang,Nanshu Wang,Deren Lei,Kai Sun,Xilun Chen,Tammy Stark,Xianhui Zhu,Yuetai Li,Rulin Shao,Tianxin Wei,Rakesh Wanga,Anuj Kumar,Jiliang Tang,Wen-tau Yih,Xin Luna Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 ProPerMem 基准（以自我中心视频＋约 61.8 万 token 的年度个人记忆为背景，含人工核验的触发窗口，联合评价内容与时机），并基于此构建 ProPerAgent：图组织记忆＋触发门控＋轻量 RL，严格准确率从不足 10% 提升到 37.5%–42.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 助手正从被动应答转向主动干预，此时记忆的作用不仅是提供答案，还要决定"是否开口、何时开口"。已有工作通常把相关事实直接放进上下文，回避了真正困难的情形：判断是否值得打扰所需的证据必须从长周期个人记忆中挖掘出来。作者希望建立一个能同时考察内容与时机、且记忆规模真实冗长的评测基准，并给出可行的解法。

Method: 构建 ProPerMem 基准：自我中心视频 + 平均约 618K token、富含干扰项的长周期用户记忆，配合人工核验的触发窗口，以及联合评分内容与时机的协议。做受控实验拆解失败原因（oracle 证据置于触发窗口内 / 由模型自行决定时机 / 用检索替换 oracle 证据）。进而提出 ProPerAgent：可插拔 harness，从图结构化的记忆库中浮现记忆，并门控到"值得打断用户"的程度，再加轻量 RL 阶段抑制过早与重复触发。

Result: 所有被评测基线表现均很差，最高仅约 15%（包括给定摘要或检索增强记忆的前沿模型）。受控实验：在触发窗口内提供 oracle 证据时 GPT-5.3-Chat 达 96%；让其自行选择时机降至 38%；将 oracle 证据替换为检索后降至 15%。ProPerAgent 无需训练即在 Qwen3.8-27B 上把严格准确率从低于 10% 提到 37.5%，在 GPT-5.3-Chat 上达 38%；RL 后训练达到 42.4%。

Conclusion: 在长周期个人记忆驱动的主动协助中，内容（证据）与时机缺一不可，任一项缺失都会造成灾难性退化，因此基准必须联合评价二者。ProPerAgent 表明：结构化的记忆浮现与触发门控是有效方向，轻量 RL 可进一步抑制早触发与重复触发带来的错误。该工作为 MPA 任务提供了基准与一套可插拔、可训练的方法基线。

Abstract: As assistants move from reactive answering toward proactive intervention, memory changes role: it no longer only supplies the answer to a query, it determines whether and when to speak at all. Prior work in this setting hands the system the relevant fact in context; we study the regime that makes the problem hard, where the evidence justifying an intervention must be mined from a long-horizon personal memory. We introduce ProPerMem, a benchmark for memory-driven proactive assistance (MPA), pairing egocentric video with year-long, distractor-rich user memories (~618K tokens on average), human-verified trigger windows, and a protocol that scores content and timing jointly. Every baseline we evaluate performs poorly (at most 15%), including frontier models given summarized or retrieval-augmented memory. A controlled study explains why: with oracle evidence delivered inside the trigger window GPT-5.3-Chat reaches 96%; letting it choose the timing drops it to 38%, and replacing oracle evidence with retrieval drops it to 15%. Content and timing are separately necessary, and missing either is catastrophic. Guided by this, we build ProPerAgent (Proactive Personalized Agent) with a plug-and-play harness that surfaces memory from a graph-organized store and gates it down to what is worth interrupting for, plus a lightweight RL stage that curbs early and repeated firing. Without any training, ProPerAgent lifts strict accuracy from below 10% to 37.5% on Qwen3.8-27B and 38% on GPT-5.3-Chat; RL post-training reaches 42.4%.

</details>


### [135] [Multimodal Filesystem Memory For Long-Horizon Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22686)
*Chloe Su,Jiahan Li,Zhenting Qi,Chaoran Cheng,Haoyu Chen,Sejoon Jun,Weirui Ye,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MFM 是一种多模态文件系统记忆架构，为长时程智能体维护情景、空间、语义/程序与工作记忆，并通过可检索文本记录连接原始多模态片段，在 Minecraft 与计算机操作任务上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体需要可复用的经验记忆，但保留全部历史难以检索相关信息，而压缩为摘要又会丢失后续所需的视觉与空间细节。

Method: 提出 MFM：情景记忆存观察、动作与结果；空间记忆将经验与位置关联；语义与程序记忆积累通用知识与可复用行为方法；工作记忆保存当前计划。用可检索的文本记录把各类表征与底层多模态情景相连，使智能体能凭上下文线索找到过往经验，并回看原始观察以恢复文本描述中缺失的细节；通过巩固机制把经验转化为高层知识与程序，同时保留对原始情景的引用。

Result: 在长时程 Minecraft 与计算机使用任务上，MFM 在三个基础模型上均带来性能提升。使用 GPT-6-Astra 时，MFM 完成 Mine Diamond from Scratch，并在 Ender Dragon 任务上达成 24 个里程碑中的 16 个，最佳基线为 12.7；在 CUABench-KiCad 上平均奖励 0.440，最佳基线 0.400，无持久记忆时为 0.267。

Conclusion: 互补的多模态记忆类型配合文本索引与原始情景回看，是兼顾可检索性与细节保真的有效长时程记忆方案；巩固机制支持经验的长期复用。

Abstract: Agents operating over long horizons need an effective memory that allows them to reuse their experience throughout long interactions. If the entire history of an interaction is retained, it becomes difficult to find the relevant experience, while compressing it into summaries may result in the loss of visual and spatial details that are required later on. We present \textbf{MFM}, a multimodal filesystem memory architecture that keeps complementary types of agent memory. \textbf{Episodic memory} stores the observations, actions, and outcomes, and \textbf{spatial memory} associates experiences with locations. \textbf{Semantic and procedural memory} build up general knowledge and reusable methods of acting, and \textbf{working memory} holds the current plans. MFM makes use of searchable textual records to connect these different representations to the underlying multimodal episodes. The agents are able to find previous experiences by means of contextual cues and to examine the original observations in order to recover details that are missing from their textual descriptions. Through consolidation, the agents convert experience into higher-level knowledge and procedures while still keeping references to the original episodes. In the case of long-horizon Minecraft and computer-use tasks, MFM leads to improved performance with three base models. With GPT-6-Astra, MFM completes \emph{Mine Diamond from Scratch} and reaches 16 out of 24 milestones on the \emph{Ender Dragon} task, as against 12.7 for the best baseline. On CUABench-KiCad it attains a mean reward of 0.440, as compared to 0.400 for the best baseline and 0.267 in the case of no persistent memory.

</details>


### [136] [Explore Separately, Adopt Selectively: Execution-Guided Tool Use over Large Tool Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22688)
*Anna Mazhar,Seiji Maekawa,Nikita Bhutani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BRIA（BRanch-Inspect-Adopt）是一种在独立分支中隔离候选工具调查过程的 LLM 智能体，能有效从大规模工具库中检索合适工具，并在性能与 token 成本上取得优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在执行任务时需要从成千上万的工具库中发现相关工具。当多个候选工具都能满足同一需求时，往往需要实际执行才能识别出合适的一个。但如果在共享轨迹中逐个尝试候选工具，会导致其他候选未被探索，且失败尝试可能干扰后续推理。

Method: 提出 BRIA 智能体，将候选工具的调查临时隔离到独立分支中。每个分支检索并执行工具以调查其候选；主任务智能体检查各分支产生的执行证据，仅将有用的结果采纳进持续轨迹中。同时，BRIA 会在任务执行过程中出现新能力需求时检索额外工具。

Result: 在 PlanBench-XL 和 MCP-Atlas 上，使用包含 1897 个工具的共享工具池、三种语言模型以及静态与自适应检索基线进行评估。在所有设置下，BRIA 均达到或超过最强检索基线。在 MCP-Atlas 上，平均 claim 得分为 89.77，而 MCP-Zero 为 75.40，提升 14.37 个百分点。BRIA 还位于任务性能与输入 token 成本的帕累托前沿上。

Conclusion: 通过在独立分支中隔离候选工具的执行调查，并仅采纳有用证据，BRIA 能有效解决共享轨迹中候选探索不足与失败干扰的问题，在大规模工具库场景下兼顾性能与成本。
